Unfortunately, however, you all know what the budgetary procedure is. An update of the budget will probably take between a month and two months, that is, within these two months, we will not have a budget with which to adequately respond to the upcoming challenges in the world. ParlaMint-BG_2022-02-25.u125	Art. 50. Proposal of the National Representative Rosen Zhelyazkov and a group of MPs with an entry number ending 44: In Article 1, paragraph 1 is replaced by the following: 50 the following amendments and additions are made: 1. In para. 1: (a) in row 1 (b) in row 2 for the carriage of passengers on non-costable lines in inland transport and in the transport in mountain and other regions. (c) A new row 3 shall be created for the carriage of passengers on long-distance bus lines other than those under § 4, para. 1 and 2 of the final provisions of the Road Transport Act......................................................................................................................................................................................................................................................... (d) the current row 3 becomes row 4; (e) in row 2. A new para is being created. 3... (3) The mayors of municipalities shall provide the funds under para. 1 row 3 of carriers which comply with the statutory order and the rules for carrying out the public transport of passengers provided for in the Road Transport Act and its implementing regulations and with which the municipality concerned has concluded contracts complying with the requirements of Regulation (EC) No 1370 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 23 October 2007 on public passenger transport services by rail and road and repealing Council Regulations (EEC) No 1191/69 and (EEC) No 1107/70. (4) The provision of para. 3 shall not apply in cases where the municipality concerned is not a contracting entity of transport under para. 3. ParlaMint-BG_2022-02-25.u181	Thank you, Mr. Chairman. Dear colleagues! I suggest we return this conversation to where it belongs, which is essentially what it is. With the other two proposals that we have made, we actually cover the whole transport sector, which is bus transport, in the cities of the electric bus, tram, metropolitan in Sofia, which provides the transport service to the citizens of the Republic of Bulgaria, which is classified as a service of general economic interest within the meaning of Regulation 1370. Within the meaning of this Regulation, the Member States of the European Union, including Bulgaria, subsidise the negative net result of the transport service carried out, namely the difference between the revenues and the costs to be made. It's actually the size that's subsidized. Copi-pass proposals in the Budget Act of recent years actually lead to us making a proposal to increase these subsidies, as I said, for Sofia Municipality on its own order, for businesses and carriers that operate on non-profit lines in cities, for those who carry out transports in remote, undercrowded and mountain areas, as well as our proposal for new 40 million for those travelling on the local lines, and the common among all these proposals is precisely the open scissors in the last two years between the reduced revenues and the increased costs. This is actually a proposal that has the character of an accidental proposal. It will not be an element of accumulation and transfer over the years because the effect of increased costs is due to two components with temporal effects. The first is the horse pandemic, which led to a reduction in own revenues due to reduced travel. For Sofia Municipality, reduced travel results in uncollected and non-generated own income of 60 million for 2020, 50 million for 2021 and expected 50 million for 2022. On the other hand, the increased costs of natural gas, electricity and diesel mainly lead to a difference in costs, which, if we consider it to be CY 2019, was around 40 million respectively, currently over 125 million. That is, this scissors currently because of two factors that we have the assurances and expectations in the second half of this year to be overcome, which, of course, has been questioned since yesterday at least because of the cost-forming factor, but these factors are of great importance for municipalities this year. Therefore, the proposals which are related to the eligibility of this expenditure without any notification, because, on the one hand, these are provided for within the meaning of the Regulation which I have quoted and within the meaning of § 4 of the Road Transport Act, which is detailed in a prescribed regulation of order, manner and conditions, both for entities and entities and for undertakings which may receive this aid. The specific estimate that is made for Sofia Municipality is related precisely to these generated costs, the reduced revenues and, of course, a commitment made by the commitments made in the budget to adequately assess and increase incomes, and this is the increase in incomes under the collective contract with employees in individual undertakings providing a transport service. (2) The powers of the Governing Council of the Fund under point (b) shall be conferred on the Board of Directors of the Fund. 1 shall be terminated from the day of the promulgation of the law in the Official Journal. The legal relations with the employees of the fund shall be governed under the conditions of art. 328, para. 1, t. 1 of the Labour Code, with the chairman of the liquidation committee exercising the rights of employer. The Prime Minister shall release the Executive Director of the closed fund. The liquidation costs, including remuneration and benefits under the Labour Code, are at the expense of the funds under the established budget of the National Housing Fund for 2022. (4) The archive of the closed fund shall be transmitted under the National Archive Fund Act. (5) Local Commissions under art. 8, para. 2 of the Law on the Settlement of the Rights of Citizens with Multiannual Housing and Savings are closed from the day of the promulgation of the Law in the Official Journal. (6) The accounts of the closed fund and of the local committees shall be closed within the time limits and by order set by the Minister of Finance. Following the closure of the accounts due in favour of the Fund, amounts shall be paid under the budget of the Ministry of Finance. (7) With the state numbers included in the total number of staff of the closed fund, the Council of Ministers, on a proposal from the Minister of Finance, shall make compensated changes in the number of the Ministry of Finance and administrations whose heads are secondary officers with budget of the Ministry of Finance. ParlaMint-BG_2022-02-25.u538	Thank you, Mr. Chairman. Colleagues, my speech will be under § 24 and § 68. I believe that for such a serious change it is necessary to pass the whole procedure with the relevant public consultation, impact assessment and the Council of Ministers to submit the bill if it believes that such a procedure should be tabled. Let me remind you, historically, the Institute of Registry Judges has always been associated with the judiciary and their figure has been introduced in our country with the Judicial Authority Act in 1994, and the judges were initially appointed without a contest by the Minister of Justice on a proposal from the President of the respective District Court, and subsequently through a competition and thus their status approaches that of the district judges. The state authorities to which the legislator has entrusted notary security functions and on the entry of acts on immovable property have always been part of the judiciary, regardless of their name, as the system of entry is essential for the development of the economy, citizens for stimulating foreign investment and for a secure and favourable economic climate and business turnover with real estate. What message would be given to the citizens of the Republic of Bulgaria, to foreign investment, to our European partners if the Institute of Registrys, an institute that has been working for 30 years in our legal system, is easily removed. On the other hand, the entry procedure should not be administrative, as the mandater will no longer have independent status but will be in power and subordination with the Director of the State Registry Agency and he will be able to exercise administrative control over the issuance of the acts and the issuance of the certificates. And therefore, again, I thank everyone, the Commission, for the fact that our proposal to reject § 24 has been accepted. As regards § 68, our motion for annulment, which is also adopted by the Commission, for which we are grateful, is extremely important that this is happening because it is unacceptable the procedural representation of the State, rather not unacceptable, but it is not appropriate in this way to make changes again and the procedural representation of the State to be carried out by the Minister of Justice instead of by the Minister of Finance. It is identical to the proposal which was announced for public consultation, which ended on 28 January 2022, according to a draft law prepared by the Ministry of Justice amending and supplementing the Civil Procedure Code without mentioning any reason. Instead of approving and submitting to the National Assembly the draft of the GDPD, the Council of Ministers has included the proposal for changes in the transitional and final provisions of the draft budget. I would like to remind you that from the liberation of Bulgaria until now the procedural representation of the state is carried out by the Minister of Finance, except in cases expressly defined by law. The cases in which the Minister of Finance is a representative of the state directly concern public finances because of the significant material interest in them, which in national cases increasingly reaches hundreds of thousands even to several million levs, in international arbitration cases can reach even more than 1 billion euros. For this reason, when the Minister of Finance represents the State in cases, he also controls the spending of public funds, including in relation to the consequences for the state budget in the event of a possible condemnation of the State. Long-term practice shows that when they are guaranteed that the amounts of judgment decisions against them will be secured by the state budget. The reasons for the bill lack any indication of problems in carrying out the procedural representation of the state by the Minister of Finance. Information on the outcome of the proceedings against the State and the Ministry of Finance is publicly available on a regular basis in the report on the implementation of the programming budget of the office available on its website. The historical reference in this regard shows that the procedural representation of the State is currently being carried out successfully and effectively. Here I should point out that the Ministry of Finance is among the few who do not use legal services in cases before the Bulgarian courts despite the number of cases, their complexity and the great material interest. The optimal organization and conduct of the protection of the state in international arbitration cases is the reason why Bulgaria has so far not lost any international investment arbitration against it. This means that the organisation of the activities in these cases now ensures optimally both the protection of public finances and contributes to building the country's image to foreign investors. The Minister of Justice, who is proposed to be entrusted with the state's procedural representation, has broad powers in the sphere of the judiciary relating to the budget of the judiciary, chamber affairs, career growth and qualification of magistrates. At the same time, the Minister of Justice presided over the plenary of the Supreme Judicial Council and carried out the overall interaction of the judiciary and executive powers. ParlaMint-BG_2022-02-25.u560	Thank you. Dear Mr. Chairman, honourable Members! I take the floor to justify one of our proposals that we have made for dropping out of the Transitional and Final Provisions of some of the paragraphs, which actually envision a serious structural reform, which we believe is not only not in its place in the Transitional and Final Provisions of the State Budget. In a nutshell, in essence, I would argue why we feel that such a reform would not be appropriate. Of course, the assessment of whether you will continue trying for this structural reform is yours, and if you re-submit these proposals where they belong in the regulatory framework, then we will take a closer look at the substance. This form provides for the integration of the Agency for Geodesy, Cartography and Cadastre, GRA and the Registry Agency. To note that geodesy and cadastral are extremely important for the country, and this mechanical transformation of the Geodesy, Cartography and Cadastral Agency in a department included in a mega agency is unacceptable without this serious discussion, which is necessary for such a reform. To add that the Agency is carrying out activities under several laws, the Law on Geodesy and Cartography, the Law on Cadastral and Property Register, the Law on Construction of the Black Sea Coast, the Law on Property and Use of Agricultural Land, the Law on Restoration of Property on Forests and Land from the Forest Fund, the Law on Construction of the Area and others. The main one, however, is the Law on Geodesy and Cartography. It defines both the tasks and objectives and the responsibilities of the respective ministers concerned with the matter of the Ministry of Regional Development and Public Works, the Ministry of Defence and the Ministry of Interior. It must not be forgotten that the Agency has been included for 20 years on the single list of strategic sites and activities that are relevant for national security. The reasons for this action are both the need to increase the security of the Cadastral Map Information System and the very high requirements for national security. I would like to point out that the Agency, if it is aimed at a symbiosis between the registers that manage these structures in the country, should note that a property register, as you all know, is not in the form in which it needs to be created. We have a register that is actually a registered register, not a property register. Such a register is to be created and it is to be the basis for the property and then the two systems are to be compatible with the property register of the Geodesy, Cartography and Cadastral Agency. At present, such a property register to be kept on the property does not exist, so I do not see the urgency of unifying the two structures. In fact, if the logic was to save the general administration, we should note that in the Agency for Geodesy, Cartography and Cadastre this general administration is not more than 10% and the other is a specialised administration. Those were the motives. Separately, I would also like to see the opinion of the CPCONPI from such a structuring. Now they have the possibility of individual registers, the manuals of the separate structures to exercise mutual control over the other structure that this register would use. Reuniting them into one structure with one subordination, I think it creates prerequisites and there is a risk. These were our motives, I am pleased that the majority of the parliamentary groups have agreed to withdraw and to drop these texts in the Transitional and Final Provisions. If you still have the idea to bring this reform into discussion, we'll get to the point. Thank you.

ParlaMint-BG_2022-02-25.u835	Dear Mr. Chairman, honourable Members! Firstly, I would like to thank you, on behalf of the entire government, for working so hard these last days, so intensely, so coordinated, and today we can boast that we have an adopted budget. This budget is unprecedented on several issues. Firstly, there are huge, large investments invested in it, investments that will start the change that we all promised. On the other hand, besides capital investments, we have investment in human capital. Unprecedentably good raises on teachers' salaries. We know that when we combine capital and human capital, we have the right formula for growth. And the last element of this budget is that this budget is social. This budget does not leave any Bulgarian to be below the poverty line. This budget shows that when we create growth and added value together, this growth and added value are distributed fairly. So thank you very much for your efforts. And what I would also like to say to all Bulgarians who are watching us right now, this budget is a success for the National Assembly, a success for the coalition. All the criticisms that were expected to be difficult, how it will succeed, this coalition has shown that there are results that what we work together is working together together. And what everyone will see with this budget, the beginning of its implementation, is the real change. Thank you. [[(Extreme applause.
